### [90] [Search for the rare Higgs boson decay H $\to$ Z$γ$ in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 and 13.6 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04402)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该论文报道了CMS实验在LHC上利用200 fb⁻¹的质子-质子对撞数据（质心能量13和13.6 TeV），对罕见的环诱导希格斯玻色子衰变H→Zγ（Z→ℓ⁺ℓ⁻）进行搜索。通过分别优化不同产生通道的灵敏度并联合拟合ℓ⁺ℓ⁻γ不变质量分布，观测到的信号强度μ=1.10（期望1.00），信号显著度为1.9个标准差（期望2.3），与标准模型预言一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: H→Zγ衰变是标准模型中由环诱导的稀有衰变过程，其分支比极小且对超出标准模型的新物理敏感。精确测量这一过程可以检验标准模型预言，并寻找可能的新物理信号。

Method: 分析使用CMS探测器记录的13和13.6 TeV pp对撞数据，总积分亮度为200 fb⁻¹。将希格斯玻色子产生通道分为胶子-胶子融合、矢量玻色子融合和伴随产生过程分别优化灵敏度，通过对各产生通道和事件类别中ℓ⁺ℓ⁻γ不变质量分布进行联合拟合来提取信号。

Result: 在希格斯玻色子质量为125.38 GeV下，观测（期望）信号强度μ=1.10（1.00），不确定度分别为+0.52/-0.61和+0.49/-0.46。信号显著性为1.9个标准差（期望2.3个标准差），结果与标准模型预言一致。

Conclusion: 未观察到超出标准模型预期的显著信号，结果与标准模型对H→Zγ衰变的预言一致，为这一稀有希格斯玻色子衰变道的测量提供了最新实验约束。

Abstract: A search is presented for the rare, loop-induced Higgs boson decay H $\to$ Z$γ$, where Z $\to$ $\ell^+\ell^-$ and $\ell$ = e or $μ$. The search is performed using a sample of proton-proton (pp) collision data at the center-of-mass energies of 13 and 13.6 TeV, recorded by the CMS experiment at the LHC and corresponding to a total integrated luminosity of 200 fb$^{-1}$. The analysis design separately considers and optimizes sensitivity to Higgs boson production via gluon-gluon fusion, vector boson fusion, and associated-production processes. The signal is extracted from a simultaneous fit to the invariant mass distributions of the $\ell^+\ell^-γ$ system in the various production channels and event categories. The observed (expected) signal strength $μ$, defined as the ratio of the measured product the of cross section and decay branching fraction value $σ$(pp $\to$ H)$\mathcal{B}$(H $\to$ Z$γ$) to the corresponding value predicted in the standard model, is found to be $μ$ = 1.10$^{+0.52}_{-0.61}$ (1.00$^{+0.49}_{-0.46}$) for a Higgs boson mass of 125.38 GeV. The signal has an observed (expected) significance of 1.9 (2.3) standard deviations.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [91] [Schatten norms and determinants of linear combinations of matrix tensor powers via virtual representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04341)
*Martin Áron Juhász,Mihály Weiner*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于表示论的方法，用于高效计算多个张量幂线性组合的Schatten范数和行列式。对于固定的矩阵维度d和项数s，该方法在n上达到多项式时间复杂度。通过Schur-Weyl对偶性获得同步分块分解，利用Jacobi-Trudi恒等式将Schur模块替换为对称幂张量积的带符号组合。当d=3时，每个不可约贡献可简化为两个对称幂项的差，并提供了开源实现。

在实际基准测试中，一个三項3×3的矩阵n=18的trace-范数问题，单线程CPU仅需约47秒计算完成，而直接存储未约简矩阵需要约2.4×10^18字节。直接计算与约简计算在n≤9范围内相对误差低于3.4×10^-15。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接计算多个矩阵张量幂线性组合X_n的行列式或Schatten范数在n上是指数级的，难以处理实际规模的n。虽然单项和两项组合可以归结为有限个标量因子，但三项及以上的一般情形缺乏类似的初等约简方法。因此需要一种精确且高效的算法能对任意项数组合进行约简，使复杂度在n上为多项式级。

Method: 利用Schur-Weyl对偶性对X_n进行同步分块分解，将问题化为对各不可约块的独立计算；在Grothendieck环中利用Jacobi-Trudi恒等式，将Schur模块展开为对称幂张量积的带符号线性组合，从而将每个不可约贡献约简为若干可显式计算的对项幂项的贡献。当d=3时，每个不可约贡献进一步简化为两个对称幂项之差，并实现了开源代码。

Result: 对于固定的d和s，算法在n上实现多项式时间。当d=3时，单线程CPU上计算真实的3×3三项n=18的trace-范数仅需约47秒，而原始矩阵储存需要约2.4×10^18字节。在n≤9的共同范围内，直接计算与约简方法结果一致，相对误差低于3.4×10^-15，验证了方法的数值精度。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了多张量幂组合（三项及以上）缺少高效精确约简算法的空白，展示了表示论工具（Schur-Weyl对偶与Jacobi-Trudi恒等式）在数值线性代数中的强大应用。所提方法在理论复杂度和实际性能上均显著优于直接暴力计算，且具备开源实现可供复现和使用。

Abstract: Let $$X_n=\sum_{i=1}^s t_i A_i^{\otimes n},$$ where $A_1,\ldots,A_s\in M_d(\mathbb C)$ and $t_1,\ldots,t_s\in\mathbb C$ are fixed, while $n$ grows. Direct computation of determinants or Schatten norms of $X_n$ is exponential in $n$. For a single tensor power these quantities are elementary, and even the determinant of a generic two-term combination admits a reduction to polynomially many scalar factors; however, no analogous elementary reduction is available for three or more terms.
  We give an exact representation-theoretic method which, for fixed $d$ and $s$, computes $\|X_n\|_p$, $0<p<\infty$, and determinants in polynomial time in $n$. Schur--Weyl duality yields a simultaneous block decomposition, while Jacobi--Trudi identities in the Grothendieck ring replace Schur modules by signed combinations of tensor products of symmetric powers.
  For $d=3$, each irreducible contribution reduces to the difference of two explicitly computable symmetric-power terms, leading to an open-source implementation. In a single-thread CPU benchmark, a genuine three-term $3\times3$ trace-norm problem with $n=18$ is evaluated in about $47$ seconds, whereas just storing the unreduced matrix would require approximately $2.4\times10^{18}$ bytes. Direct and reduced computations agree to relative error below $3.4\times10^{-15}$ throughout their common range $n\leq9$.

</details>


### [92] [On a basepoint issue in the tempered dual of $p$-adic $SL(n)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05055)
*Marie Dautheville*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究非阿基米德局部域上SL(n,F)的Levi子群M的离散序列在酉非分歧特征群扭转作用下的Weyl群稳定子作用。构造了当n≥8且非素数、非9时无不动点的轨道族，同时证明素数维数和小维数情形总是存在不动点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题源于表示论中关于酉非分歧特征作用下的轨道与其Weyl稳定子之间的不动点问题，这可能与Langlands纲领、A-封装以及其他关于离散序列参数的调和分析问题密切相关。作者希望厘清哪些参数维数存在无不动点的轨道，从而更好地理解表示论中的结构性质。

Method: 作者构造了M具有m个大小为k≥2的块和2个大小为m的块这样结构的Levi子群，并利用超尖表示（supercuspidal representations）构造技术，通过构造满足特定条件的GL(m,F)和GL(k,F)的超尖表示来构建目标轨道族。

Result: 对任意整除n且满足m | q-1的除数m≥2（其中n≥8非素数且不为9），构造了W_𝒪作用无不动点的轨道族。同时证明了被排除的维数情形（素数维数及小维数）下W_𝒪作用总是存在不动点。

Conclusion: 该研究为SL(n,F)离散序列在酉非分歧特征扭转下的不动点问题提供了完整的刻画：除素数维数和小维数外，对非素数且非9的n≥8，存在无不动点的轨道族；而在素数和小维数情形则总有不动点。

Abstract: Let $F$ be a non-archimedean local field of characteristic zero. Let $M$ be a Levi subgroup of $G = \mathrm{SL}(n,F)$. Consider the action on the discrete series of $M$ of the group of unitary unramified characters of $M$ (where a character acts by twisting). Given an orbit $\mathcal{O}$ for that action, let $W_\mathcal{O}$ be the global stabilizer of $\mathcal{O}$ in the Weyl group of $M$. We construct families of orbits $\mathcal{O}$ for which the action of $W_\mathcal{O}$ has no fixed point.
  More precisely, let $n \geq 8$ be an integer that is not prime and not equal to $9$. We attach families of orbits without fixed points to any divisor $m \geq 2$ of $n$ such that $m \;| \; q-1$ where $q$ is the order of the residue field of $F$. The Levi subgroup $M \subset G$ for the corresponding families has $m$ blocks of size $k\geq 2$ and $2$ blocks of size $m$. The core of the paper is the construction of superculpidal representations of $\mathrm{GL}(m,F)$ and $\mathrm{GL}(k,F)$ satisfying some conditions that we require to construct the families of orbits $\mathcal{O}$ that we expects.
  In addition, we study the fixed point problem for the dimensions $n$ that we excluded above, i.e., prime or small dimensions. We prove that there is always a fixed point under the action of $W_\mathcal{O}$ in those cases.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文在五配位枫叶晶格和格子晶格上构造了具有键依赖各向异性相互作用的自旋模型，实现了与Kitaev蜂窝模型类似的精确Z₂量子自旋液体，并通过量子蒙特卡洛模拟和解析分析系统地表征了其基态性质、拓扑相图和任意子激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kitaev蜂窝模型提供了精确可解的自旋液体平台，但该模型的晶格几何较为有限。作者希望在五配位晶格（枫叶晶格和格子晶格）上推广Kitaev型键依赖各向异性相互作用，探索更丰富晶格几何下量子自旋液体与拓扑序的存在性，并克服奇数长度基本口袋（plaquette）上简并微扰论的困难。

Method: 1) 在枫叶晶格和格子晶格上构造具有键依赖各向异性相互作用的精确可解自旋模型；2) 采用flux sector、Chern数和拓扑激发对基态进行分类；3) 使用副本交换量子蒙特卡洛（replica exchange QMC）模拟研究低温下规范通量的序；4) 利用大"dimer"极限建立涡旋激发与两类任意子之间的对应关系；5) 推导Majorana费米子质量项的解析表达式，其消失标志着拓扑相变。

Result: 模拟表明在低温下规范通量有序：每个单元三角形携带±π/2-flux且符号在整个系统中均匀，每个单元六边形为0-flux，正方形为π-flux。绘制的拓扑相图揭示了Z₂拓扑序和Ising拓扑序区域。发现枫叶模型的两个dimer极限（具有不同的任意子种类分配）在无涡旋扇区可以平滑连接，但在某些双涡旋扇区被费米子能隙闭合转变所分隔。

Conclusion: 该工作将Kitaev型精确可解自旋液体从蜂窝晶格推广至五配位枫叶和格子晶格，建立了这些晶格上Z₂与Ising拓扑序之间的相图及相变机制，并通过dimer极限提供了处理奇数长度基本口袋的涡旋-任意子对应方案，为理解更复杂晶格几何下的量子自旋液体和拓扑序奠定了基础。

Abstract: We construct spin models with bond-dependent anisotropic interactions on the penta-coordinated maple-leaf and trellis lattices, which yield exact $\mathbb{Z}_2$ quantum spin liquids akin to the Kitaev honeycomb model. We characterize the resulting ground states by their flux sectors, Chern numbers, and topological excitations. Using replica exchange quantum Monte Carlo simulations, we find that the gauge fluxes are ordered at sufficiently low temperatures such that every unit triangle has $\pm π/2$-flux, where the sign is uniform across the system, and every unit hexagon (square) has $0$-flux ($π$-flux) within the parameter space of interest. We map out the topological phase diagram for each model, which reveals parameter regimes hosting $\mathbb{Z}_2$ and Ising topological orders, and we derive an analytical expression for the mass term of the Majorana fermions, the vanishing of which indicates a transition between these phases. We further establish the correspondence between individual vortices (i.e., flux excitations) and two species of anyons in the dimer limit, overcoming the obstacle faced by degenerate perturbation theory in treating odd-length elementary plaquettes. Interestingly, we find that two dimer limits of the maple-leaf model with distinct assignments of anyon species can be smoothly connected to each other in the vortex-free sector, but they are separated by fermion-gap-closing transitions in certain two-vortex sectors.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电荷密度波（CDW）是电子电荷密度周期性调制与晶格畸变耦合形成的宏观量子态，尽管已有大量研究，但在CDW存在下电磁输运性质的演化仍然缺乏深入探索，需要系统研究多CDW共存体系中的输运行为。

Method: 在DyTe3这一准二维材料中，通过低温下的电子输运测量，研究了两个正交CDW（CDW1和CDW2）共存时的磁输运和霍尔响应行为，分别考察两个CDW转变对磁阻和霍尔信号的影响。

Result: 实验条件下未在CDW1转变处观察到明确的磁输运异常；而CDW2的发生导致了磁阻的剧烈增强以及由多带效应主导的非线性霍尔响应。这表明CDW1和CDW2在DyTe3中对电子输运的影响完全不同。

Conclusion: DyTe3中CDW1和CDW2本质不同，对电子输运产生定性不同的影响，使DyTe3成为理解准二维材料中多重CDW输运后果的模型体系。

Abstract: Charge-density-waves (CDWs) are macroscopic quantum states defined by periodic modulations in electronic charge density coupled with lattice distortions. Despite significant research efforts, the evolution of electromagnetic transport properties in the presence of CDWs remains largely unexplored. Here, we report the effects of two orthogonal CDWs (CDW1 and CDW2) coexisting at low-temperature in DyTe3 on its electronic transport properties. Importantly, we find that while no clear magnetotransport anomaly is resolved across CDW1 transition under the present experimental conditions, the emergence of CDW2 drives a drastic enhancement in magnetoresistance and a multiband-governed nonlinear Hall response. These results demonstrate that CDW1 and CDW2 in DyTe3 are fundamentally different and impact its electronic transport in qualitatively distinct ways, establishing DyTe3 as a model system for understanding the transport consequences of multiple CDWs in quasi-two-dimensional materials.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文利用Ni L3边和O K边RIXS对层状镍氧化物La3Ni2O7的两种多晶型（交替单层-三层LNO-1313和双层LNO-2222）以及三层化合物La4Ni3O10进行了系统比较研究，发现LNO-1313与La4Ni3O10在电子、磁性和晶格激发上高度相似，而双层LNO-2222表现出显著不同的特征，并在有效局域矩框架下提出纠缠二聚体模型来描述强反铁磁层间耦合产生的自旋激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ruddlesden-Popper镍氧化物超导电性的发现引发了核心问题：层状架构如何影响与配对各相关的电子、磁性和晶格相互作用。理解不同层状排列对超导机制的影响具有关键意义。

Result: 发现LNO-1313与La4Ni3O10具有类似的光谱特征，而LNO-2222呈现不同特征。LNO-1313和La4Ni3O10相对LNO-2222具有更弱的轨道极化、更强的3d8L特征、更小的面外磁交换尺度以及更强的电子-声子耦合（EPC）。在有效局域矩框架下，纠缠二聚体场景能自然描述由强反铁磁层间耦合产生的自旋激发。

Conclusion: 这些对比发现为理解层状镍氧化物中超导相关的低能物理提供了关键的实验约束，其中纠缠二聚体模型相比传统自旋波理论在双层LNO-2222中表现出明显优势，为未来理论模型构建提供了重要依据。

Abstract: The discovery of superconductivity in Ruddlesden-Popper nickelates has raised a central question: how does layer architecture shape the electronic, magnetic, and lattice interactions relevant to pairing? Here, we report a detailed comparative study of the two polymorphs of La3Ni2O7--the alternating monolayer-trilayer (LNO-1313) and bilayer (LNO-2222) structures--and the related trilayer compound La4Ni3O10, using both Ni L3- and O K-edge RIXS. We find that LNO-1313 and La4Ni3O10 share strikingly similar electronic, magnetic, and lattice excitations, whereas bilayer LNO-2222 exhibits distinct features. Compared to LNO-2222, LNO-1313 and La4Ni3O10 have weaker orbital polarization, enhanced 3d8L character, a reduced out-of-plane magnetic-exchange scale, and stronger EPC. Within an effective local-moment framework, an entangled-dimer scenario provides a natural description of the spin excitations generated by strong antiferromagnetic interlayer coupling. Its advantage over conventional spin-wave theory is clearest in bilayer LNO-2222, where the interlayer coupling dominates the intralayer interactions. These findings provide critical experimental constraints for future theoretical models for the low-energy physics relevant to superconductivity in these layered nickelates.

</details>

*Hakan Yeler,Özgür Çakır*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用TEBD方法求解Lindblad主方程，研究了一维Fermi-Hubbard链与边界粒子库及局部去相位浴耦合下的稳态输运。在无去相位时，电流随边界驱动率呈现两个分离的极大值，反映强关联区分离的电荷与自旋能标。引入去相位后发现两种不同的去相位诱导输运增强机制，分析哈密顿本征基下的非平衡稳态表明这两种机制分别源于上Hubbard带内的重分布和Hubbard带间的跃迁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索强关联开放量子系统中，多体关联如何影响相干驱动、去相位与量子Zeno物理之间的相互作用，特别是去相位对输运增强效应的影响，此前仅针对无自旋费米子系统的单一增强机制已有研究，而含自旋的Hubbard模型中是否存在更丰富的现象尚不清楚。

Method: 采用时间演化块消减（TEBD）方法数值求解Lindblad主方程，对一维Fermi-Hubbard链分别耦合边界粒子库（提供相干驱动）和每个格点的局域去相位浴进行稳态输运模拟。通过在哈密顿本征基下分析非平衡稳态的粒子分布，揭示去相位诱导的重分布机制。

Result: 无去相位时电流出现两个分离的极大值（对应电荷与自旋能标）；引入去相位后，观察到两种截然不同的去相位诱导输运增强区域，优于自旋无费米子系统中此前报道的单一增强机制。本征基分析表明两种增强分别源于上Hubbard带内的粒子重分布以及Hubbard带间的跃迁过程。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了多体关联（自旋-电荷分离）在强关联开放系统中塑造去相位增强输运的多种通道，加深了对相干驱动、去相位和量子Zeno效应协同作用的理解，为强关联耗散系统的输运调控提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study steady-state transport in a one-dimensional Fermi-Hubbard chain coupled to particle reservoirs at the boundaries and to local dephasing baths at each site, using the time-evolving block decimation (TEBD) method to solve the Lindblad master equation. In the absence of dephasing, the current exhibits two well-separated maxima as a function of the boundary driving rate, reflecting the distinct charge and spin energy scales of the strongly correlated regime. Upon introducing dephasing, we find two distinct dephasing-induced transport-enhancement regimes, in contrast to the single enhancement previously reported for spinless fermions. Analysis of the non-equilibrium steady state in the Hamiltonian eigenbasis reveals that the two regimes originate from distinct dephasing-induced redistribution processes: the first involves redistribution within the uppermost Hubbard band, while the second involves transitions between Hubbard bands. Our results demonstrate how many-body correlations shape the interplay between coherent driving, dephasing, and quantum Zeno physics in strongly correlated open systems.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We present a systematic numerical study of the spin-1 antiferromagnetic Heisenberg model on the triangular lattice in an out-of-plane magnetic field, using Density Matrix Renormalization Group (DMRG) methods. By mapping out the quantum phase diagram as a function of the single-ion anisotropy $D_z$ and magnetic field, we identify distinct dipolar and quadrupolar spin supersolid states, characterized by spontaneous U(1) symmetry breaking with finite spin superfluid stiffness coexisting with longitudinal translational symmetry breaking. At zero field, the dipolar spin supersolid with a 'Y'-type spin configuration persists down to $D_z = 0$, whereas the quadrupolar spin supersolid prevails at large $D_z$. At intermediate fields, the phase diagram is dominated by an up-up-down phase. At high fields below saturation, a quadrupolar spin superfluid emerges in the large-$D_z$ regime, whereas a dipolar spin supersolid with a 'V'-type spin configuration dominates at small $D_z$. These phases are characterized through their order parameters and spin superfluid stiffness using calculations on various system sizes. Furthermore, the dynamical spin structure factor is obtained across the phase diagram, where characteristic spectral signatures of different phases are observed, including the gapless Goldstone mode and the roton-like minima. These features are directly accessible to inelastic neutron scattering experiments. Our results provide a theoretical understanding of the interplay between frustrations, anisotropy, and Zeeman interactions in driving distinct spin supersolid phases in the spin-1 system, which are relevant to various triangular-lattice antiferromagnets such as Na$_2$BaNi(PO$_4$)$_2$ and K$_2$Ni(SeO$_3$)$_2$.

</details>


### [98] [Breakdown of Anomalous Hall Scaling in Dilute Kondo System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05319)
*Arnab Bhattacharya,Prashant Singh,Ajay Kumar,Afsar Ahmed,Yaroslav Mudryk,I. Das,Anis Biswas*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文报道了非f电子Heusler合金Ru₂Mn₀.₅Ti₀.₅Ge中的本征Berry曲率驱动的反常霍尔输运，证明平带范霍夫奇点与Kondo杂化、自旋轨道耦合共同产生Berry曲率，且Kondo相干对Berry曲率相关的铁性响应有深远影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强电子关联与Berry曲率驱动的输运现象的交叉领域尚未被充分探索，特别是来源于平带物理而非传统f电子态的电子关联系统。作者希望填补这一空白，探究平带物理驱动的强关联体系中拓扑性质与反常输运的内在联系。

Method: 实验上在稀释Kondo体系Ru₂Mn₀.₅Ti₀.₅Ge中测量反常霍尔效应，观察其随温度的非单调行为；理论上利用第一性原理（ab initio）计算，分析Kondo杂化诱导的平带与自旋轨道耦合介导的能带反交叉对Berry曲率的贡献。

Result: 发现该材料的反常霍尔响应呈现显著非单调行为，在磁转变附近违反费米液体标度关系，仅在低温下随Kondo相干开始而恢复。第一性原理计算证实Berry曲率源自Kondo杂化诱导的平带与自旋轨道耦合反交叉在费米能级处的相互作用。

Conclusion: 平带Kondo相干对Berry曲率相关的铁性响应具有深刻影响，表明在倒空间拓扑与强关联内在交织的体系中，反常输运需要更严格的理论理解。该工作拓展了对非f电子体系中关联诱导拓扑响应的认知。

Abstract: The confluence of strong electronic correlations and Berry curvature-driven transport constitutes a largely underexplored frontier in quantum materials research, particularly in systems where electronic correlations arise from flat-band physics rather than conventional $f$-electron states. Here, we report an intrinsic Berry curvature driven anomalous Hall transport in the dilute Kondo system Ru$_2$Mn$_{0.5}$Ti$_{0.5}$Ge, a non-$f$-electron Heusler alloy hosting flat bands with van Hove singularity proximate to the Fermi level. The anomalous Hall response is strikingly non-monotonic, violating the Fermi-liquid scaling relation near the magnetic transition and is restored only at low temperature, coincident with the onset of Kondo coherence. \textit{Ab initio} calculations strongly establish that the Berry curvature originates from the Kondo hybridization induced interplay of flat bands and spin-orbit coupling mediated anticrossings at the Fermi level. Our findings demonstrate that Kondo coherence of flat bands bears a profound impact on Berry curvature associated ferroic responses, mandating a rigorous theoretical understanding of anomalous transport in the regime where reciprocal space topology and strong correlations are intrinsically intertwined.

</details>

### [99] [Improving the efficiency of a Hyperpower method for approximating inverse matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04433)
*Alicia Cordero,Juan R. Torregrosa*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文首次将标量范数型加速器用于求解矩阵方程 X^{-1}-A=0，在不增加矩阵-矩阵乘法计算量的前提下，将已知迭代方法的收敛阶提升50%，实现以四阶Hyperpower方法的计算成本获得六阶收敛。作者证明了收敛性和稳定性，并在多达10^6条目的规模矩阵上与四阶、六阶和八阶方法对比验证了良好数值表现，最后将其应用于数字图像恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高求解逆矩阵方程迭代方法的收敛阶时，通常以增加矩阵-矩阵乘法的计算成本为代价。作者希望通过引入标量范数型加速器这一新手段，在减少矩阵-矩阵乘法数量的同时提升收敛阶数，从而获得更高的计算效率。

Method: 提出一种新的标量范数型加速器技术，将其嵌入已知的迭代方法中，使得收敛阶从四阶提升到六阶（提升50%），但矩阵-矩阵乘积的计算成本仍保持与四阶Hyperpower方法相当。作者推导并证明了新方案的收敛阶、效率和稳定性。

Result: 新方法实现了以四阶方法的计算代价获得六阶收敛，并在含有10^6个元素的大型矩阵上与现有四阶、六阶和八阶迭代方法进行了数值对比，表现出良好的精度和效率。在数字图像恢复应用中同样展现了优于现有方法的性能。

Conclusion: 该标量范数型加速器技术开辟了一种新型迭代过程设计途径，具有良好性能与可扩展性，不仅限于当前求解矩阵求逆的框架，还可推广至更广泛的数值迭代方法中。

Abstract: In this manuscript, scalar norm-type accelerators are used for first time in order to increase the order of convergence of known iterative methods for solving the matrix equation $X^{-1}-A=0$, while decreasing the number of matrix-matrix products. Specifically, our proposed scheme upgrades the order of convergence in a $50\%$, achieving the sixth-order of convergence with the computational cost of fourth-order Hyperpower method, in terms of matrix-matrix multiplications. In the main results, not only the convergence and its order is proven, but also its efficiency and stability. This new technique provides good numerical results, even compared with existing fourth-, sixth- and eighth-order schemes in large matrices of up to $10^6$ entries, but also promises the opening of a new kind of procedures with good performance and scalability. Finally, an application to digital image restoration is made to show the behavior of the method, compared with existing ones.

</details>


### [100] [Adaptive Multilevel Discontinuous Galerkin Methods on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04534)
*Michał Wichrowski*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于GPU的矩阵自由对称内罚间断伽辽金方法，用于自适应加密笛卡尔网格。通过辅助"影子单元"将非匹配界面转化为匹配面，并构建局部几何多重网格加速求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应加密的笛卡尔网格在非匹配界面处存在多项式阶次不一致的问题（粗网格相邻细网格），导致面求值难以统一处理。同时需要设计适合GPU并行执行的高效求解器。

Method: 在细网格层引入辅助影子单元表示相邻粗网格多项式，将非匹配界面转化为匹配面。证明该构造与标准非匹配公式等价。利用此表示构建局部几何多重网格，冻结的影子提供层间边界数据并携带残差贡献到粗层。通过原始-对偶配对消除Krylov迭代中的影子组装。

Result: 在NVIDIA A100上采用三次元(p=3)进行数值实验，结果表明随着加密深度增加多重网格收敛稳定，GPU执行高效。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了GPU上自适应网格的非匹配界面统一处理，并通过影子单元与多重网格结合获得了稳定的收敛性与高效的GPU性能。

Abstract: I present a matrix-free symmetric interior penalty discontinuous Galerkin method for adaptively refined Cartesian meshes on GPUs. At non-matching interfaces, auxiliary \emph{shadow cells} represent the adjacent coarse polynomial on the fine level. This approach converts non-matching interfaces into matching faces, permitting uniform face evaluation throughout the mesh. I prove that this construction is equivalent to the standard non-matching formulation. Furthermore, this representation yields a local geometric multigrid method in which frozen shadows provide inter-level boundary data and carry residual contributions to coarser levels. A primal--dual pairing eliminates shadow assembly from the Krylov iteration. Numerical experiments with cubic elements ($p=3$) on an NVIDIA A100 show stable multigrid convergence under increasing refinement depth and efficient GPU execution.

</details>


### [101] [A Complete Resolution of Forsythe's Conjecture for Restarted Conjugate Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04659)
*Matthew J. Colbrook,George Stepaniants,Alex Townsend*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文完全解决了Forsythe猜想（1968年提出），该猜想断言对于每个重启长度s，精确算术的重启共轭梯度（CG）迭代在实对称正定问题上的归一化残差沿偶数和奇数重启子序列分别收敛。通过对重启长度进行完整分类，发现该猜想的普适结论仅在s∈{1,2,3}时成立，对每个s≥4均存在维度为s+4的对角正定反例，其偶数残差方向不收敛。这一结果终结了近六十年来悬而未决的渐近性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Forsythe猜想自1968年提出以来，除了经典的steepest-descent情形（s=1）由Akaike定理解决外，该渐近问题在完全一般性上悬而未决近六十年。作者旨在对原始有限维设定下的重启长度进行完整分类，并识别出从收敛到发散转变的尖锐阈值。

Method: （1）通过一个不依赖次数的双正交恒等式以及低次不动点集的分析，证明s=2和s=3的正面结果；（2）在重启长度s=4时，利用有理区间算术和Sturm序列证明重标定平方权重映射的前导向量场存在横向Hopf点；（3）通过解析周期轨道和阴影（shadowing）论证构造重启长度为4的反例；（4）利用次数提升（degree elevation）技术将反例推广到所有更大的重启长度s≥4。

Result: 完整的分发结果为：对于s=2和s=3，每个问题要么终止，要么偶数和奇数残差方向均收敛；对于每个s≥4，存在维度为s+4的对角正定反例，该反例永不终止且偶数残差方向不收敛。结合Akaike关于s=1的定理，证明了猜想成立当且仅当s∈{1,2,3}，对一切s≥4均不成立。

Conclusion: 该工作对Forsythe的渐近猜想给出了完整的最终裁决：收敛性仅在s≤3时成立，从s=4开始即被破坏。识别出阈值s=4是关键贡献，所发展的双正交恒等式、Hopf点验证及周期轨道构造等工具为该类迭代算法的渐近分析提供了新的方法论。

Abstract: Forsythe's conjecture, published in 1968, asserts that for each restart length $s$, every exact-arithmetic restarted conjugate-gradient iteration on a real symmetric positive definite problem either terminates or has normalised residuals that converge separately along the even and odd restart subsequences. Apart from the classical steepest-descent case, this asymptotic question remained unresolved in full generality for nearly six decades. We give a complete classification by restart length in the original finite-dimensional setting and identify a sharp threshold. For $s=2$ and $s=3$, every problem either terminates or has convergent even and odd residual directions. For every $s\ge4$, there is a diagonal positive definite counterexample of dimension $s+4$ which never terminates and whose even residual directions do not converge. Together with Akaike's theorem for $s=1$, this shows that the conjectured universal conclusion is true precisely for $s\in\{1,2,3\}$ and false for every $s\ge4$. The positive results follow from a degree-independent double-orthogonality identity and an analysis of the low-degree fixed-point sets. At restart length four, rational interval arithmetic and Sturm sequences certify a transverse Hopf point of the leading vector field of the rescaled squared-weight map. Analytic periodic-orbit and shadowing arguments yield the counterexample at restart length four, and degree elevation extends the construction to every larger restart length.

</details>


### [102] [A class of high-order discontinuous-Galerkin methods satisfying infinitely many entropy conditions with provable error estimates and strong convergence for general nonlinear conservation laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04687)
*Yuanzhe Wei,Chi-Wang Shu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用算子半群推导半离散间断伽辽金方法的新框架，用于标量守恒律，并基于此构造了一类在非均匀网格上满足无穷多局部熵不等式的高阶OFDG型格式（使用一般E通量），进一步推广到任意空间维度的守恒律方程组，并为光滑解和非连续解分别证明了最优误差估计和强收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在克服传统DG方法在守恒律应用中熵稳定性和收敛性理论方面的不足，通过算子半群这一新视角建立统一的理论框架，从而设计同时满足高阶精度、熵稳定性和强收敛保障的数值格式。

Method: 作者利用算子半群理论推导半离散DG方法，构造了一类高阶OFDG型格式，该格式使用一般E通量并且在非均匀网格上满足无穷多局部熵不等式。随后将该框架推广到任意空间维度的守恒律方程组，采用基于文献[1]意义的熵稳定数值通量，并使用补偿紧致（compensated compactness）方法对严格凸标量守恒律的非连续解证明强收敛性。

Result: 该框架成功构造了满足无穷多局部熵不等式的高阶OFDG型格式（非均匀网格上），并推广到多维守恒律方程组；对非线性标量守恒律的光滑解证明了最优误差估计，对严格凸守恒律的非连续解通过补偿紧致方法证明了强收敛性；同时对一般E通量也给出了理论保证。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个基于算子半群的通用框架来推导和设计熵稳定的高阶DG型格式，在理论上确保了格式的熵稳定性、最优收敛阶以及针对非连续解的强收敛性，为该类数值方法提供了坚实的数学理论基础。

Abstract: We propose a novel framework for deriving semi-discrete discontinuous-Galerkin (DG) methods using operator semigroups for scalar conservation laws, then apply it to construct a class of high-order OFDG-type schemes [13] satisfying infinitely many local entropy inequalities with general E-fluxes on non-uniform meshes. Such schemes are further generalized to systems of conservation laws in any number of space dimensions by using entropy stable numerical fluxes in the sense of [1]. Finally, we prove optimal error estimates for smooth solutions to nonlinear scalar conservation laws, and prove strong convergence for discontinuous solutions to strictly convex conservation laws via compensated compactness.

</details>


### [103] [Energy-Consistent Splitting and Decomposition Approaches for Coupled port-Hamiltonian ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04884)
*Marius Mönch,Nicole Marheineke,Andreas Bartel,Kevin Schäfers,Michael Günther*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了算子分裂方法在耦合港口哈密顿系统数值积分中的应用，提出了"能量一致性"作为评估分裂方法的标准，推导了分裂继承连续系统能量行为的充分条件，并通过数值实验比较了多种分解策略的效率与能量特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算子分裂是求解耦合港口哈密顿系统的吸引人方法，它能在子问题层面利用系统结构。然而，分解的选择不唯一，且会显著影响计算效率和能量行为保持。此前缺乏对二者相互作用的系统研究，需要建立统一的评估准则。

Method: 提出能量一致性作为评估港口哈密顿常微分方程分裂方法的标准；推导分裂继承连续系统能量行为的充分条件；基于这些条件分析多种分解策略，包括保持结构的分解、利用低维子系统动力学的分解和利用时间尺度分离的分解，并使用Strang分裂及其多时间步扩展进行数值实验验证。

Result: 数值结果表明，保持港口哈密顿子流结构是能量一致分裂的关键；而利用子系统结构或时间尺度分离的分解可带来显著计算优势。特别是时间尺度分解对具有明显多带速率特征的系统能产生显著的效率增益；破坏结构的分裂可能导致不良的能量行为。

Conclusion: 保持港口哈密顿结构对于能量一致的分裂至关重要，但结构保持与计算效率之间存在权衡。时间尺度分解在具有多带速率特性的系统中能兼顾效率与能量准确性，为耦合港口哈密顿系统的高效数值积分提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Operator splitting provides an attractive approach for the numerical integration of (coupled) port-Hamiltonian systems, as it allows the underlying system structure to be exploited at the level of the individual subproblems. However, the choice of the decomposition is not unique and may strongly affect both the computational efficiency and the preservation of the energy behavior of the original system. In this work, we investigate this interplay systematically and introduce energy consistency as a criterion for assessing splitting methods for port-Hamiltonian ordinary differential equations. We derive sufficient conditions under which a splitting based on a given decomposition inherits the energy behavior of the continuous system and use these conditions to analyze several decomposition strategies for coupled port-Hamiltonian systems. In particular, we compare decompositions that preserve the structure with approaches that exploit lower-dimensional subsystem dynamics or separated time scales. The analysis is complemented by numerical experiments using Strang splitting and its multiple-time-stepping extension. A scalable electro-thermal benchmark with fast electrical and slow thermal dynamics is employed to assess accuracy, energy behavior, and computational efficiency. The results demonstrate that preserving the port-Hamiltonian structure of the subflows is essential for energy-consistent splitting, whereas decompositions that exploit subsystem structure or time-scale separation can provide substantial computational advantages. In particular, the time-scale decomposition yields significant efficiency gains for systems with pronounced multirate characteristics, while structure-destroying decompositions may lead to undesirable energy behavior.

</details>


### [104] [Construction of trace-preserving Fortin operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04926)
*Franziska Eickmann,Tabea Tscherpel*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架，用于为Stokes方程的协调混合有限元对构造局部Fortin算子。该算子满足散度保持、局部稳定性、逼近性质及某些迹保持性质，并针对多种有限元对（包括P_d-P_0元、Bernardi-Raugel元、协调Crouzeix-Raviart元、修正MINI元和广义Taylor-Hood元）给出了具体构造。文中还讨论了此类Fortin算子存在性在inf-sup稳定性之外的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fortin算子是证明混合有限元方法inf-sup稳定性的关键工具，但现有的Fortin算子构造往往是针对特定元素逐一进行的，缺乏统一框架。此外，传统的Fortin算子通常只关注inf-sup稳定性，而忽略了散度保持和迹保持等额外性质，这些性质对于处理非牛顿流体、不均匀Dirichlet边界条件以及移动域等问题至关重要。

Method: 本文提出一个统一的构造框架，以系统性地为协调混合有限元对设计局部（局部化）Fortin算子。所构造的算子需同时满足多个关键性质：散度保持性（divergence-preservation）、局部稳定性、逼近性质以及迹保持性质。对于某些有限元对，为了满足迹保持性质，需要对速度空间进行富集（enrichment）。文中将该框架依次应用于P_d-P_0元、Bernardi-Raugel元、协调Crouzeix-Raviart元、修正MINI元和广义Taylor-Hood元等具体元素。

Result: 成功为上述多种经典及较新的混合有限元对构造了满足所需性质的局部Fortin算子，并展示了当某些元素（针对迹保持性质）需要对速度空间进行富集的具体情况。同时，文章指出这些加强的Fortin算子的存在使得混合有限元方法可以超越单纯的inf-sup稳定性分析，应用于更广泛的场景。

Conclusion: 该统一框架为多种混合有限元提供了具有更丰富结构性质的Fortin算子，其意义不仅限于验证inf-sup稳定性：它还使得这些元可以应用于非牛顿流体流动问题、含非均匀Dirichlet边界条件的问题，以及涉及域逼近和移动域的均匀inf-sup稳定性分析等更广泛的场景。

Abstract: We present a unifying framework to construct local Fortin operators for conforming mixed finite element pairs for the Stokes equations. The operators are constructed to satisfy the divergence-preservation property, local stability and approximation properties, and certain trace-preservation properties. For the latter, some of the finite element pairs require an enrichment of the velocity space. We present the construction for the $P_d-P_0$ element, the Bernardi-Raugel element, the conforming Crouzeix-Raviart element, a modified MINI element and generalised Taylor-Hood elements. Furthermore, we discuss implications of the existence of such a Fortin operator beyond inf-sup stability. These include applications to non-Newtonian fluid flow, problems with inhomogeneous Dirichlet boundary conditions, as well as uniform inf-sup stability relevant for domain approximation and moving domains.

</details>


### [105] [Frequency-explicit convergence analysis of a multiscale finite element method for highly heterogeneous scattering problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04930)
*T. Chaumont-Frelet,Z. Kassali*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对高频时间谐波散射中高度异质可穿透障碍物的数值逼近问题，提出了一种高阶多尺度有限元方法，并给出了显式依赖于波数k和材料特征尺寸ε的误差分析。理论分析表明高阶方法在高频大尺度问题中能降低计算成本，数值实验验证了这一结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频区（kL >> 1）下高度异质可穿透障碍物的时间谐波散射数值逼近极具挑战性，特别是当障碍物包含特征尺度ε满足kε << 1的多材料结构时。现有的数值方法在高频区间计算成本高昂，缺乏兼顾k和ε的显式误差分析，亟需开发高效方法。

Method: 提出一种高阶多尺度有限元方法（high-order multiscale finite element method），对含多尺度材料结构的散射问题进行数值离散，并构建同时显式依赖波数k和材料特征尺寸ε的误差估计框架。

Result: 误差估计表明高阶方法在高频大尺度情形下可降低计算成本（减少自由度或提高收敛阶），数值算例验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 高阶多尺度有限元方法是处理高频、高度异质可穿透散射问题的有效途径，其误差分析与k和ε的显式依赖关系为实际计算中的参数选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We analyze the numerical approximation of time-harmonic scattering by highly heterogeneous penetrable obstacles. These problems are especially challenging in the high-frequency regime, where the size of the scatterer $L$ is much larger than the wavelength, i.e., the wavenumber $k$ is such that $kL \gg 1$. Here, we further consider the situation where the scatterer contains different materials, with a characteristic size $\varepsilon$ such that $k\varepsilon \ll 1$. We propose a high-order multiscale finite element method, and provide an error analysis that is explicit in both $k$ and $\varepsilon$. Crucially, our error estimates suggest that using a high-order method should reduce the computational cost for large frequencies, which is corroborated by numerical examples.

</details>


### [106] [A simple shallow neural network for emulating the solution to singularly perturbed problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05180)
*Christos Xenophontos,Aayushman Raina*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种浅层神经网络方法，用于模拟具有两个小参数的奇异摄动二阶边值问题的解。该方法利用解的渐近展开分解（光滑部分和边界层部分），通过tanh激活函数并增强指数函数来扩展逼近空间，实现参数鲁棒的数值模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异摄动问题在多个小参数下解具有边界层特征，传统神经网络方法难以有效逼近这类具有陡峭梯度变化的解。作者希望利用已知的渐近展开先验信息来设计神经网络架构，使其能够参数鲁棒地模拟此类问题。

Method: 利用解的渐近展开进行加性分解（光滑部分与层分量），获得关于微分阶数和摄动参数显式的导数估计；基于此分解设计浅层神经网络，采用tanh激活函数，将合适的指数函数增强到逼近空间中（类似于有限元中的富集空间方法）；采用不同的训练目标，如残差最小化或能量最小化。

Result: 一维和二维的数值实验（包括光滑非张量积域）表明，该方法在测试的摄动参数范围内表现出参数鲁棒的行为。

Conclusion: 通过将渐近展开先验信息融入神经网络架构（增强指数函数），所提出的神经网络方法能够有效且参数鲁棒地模拟多参数奇异摄动边值问题的解。

Abstract: We consider (feed-forward) Neural Networks (NNs) for the emulation of the solution to singularly perturbed second order boundary value problems, with two small parameters. We describe a shallow NN which exploits available asymptotic expansions for the solution. These additive decompositions into smooth and layer components, allow for derivative estimates which are explicit in the order of differentiation as well as the singular perturbation parameter(s) \cite{melenk, Irene, SX}. Utilizing such decompositions, we propose a simple NN for emulating the solution to such problems using the $\tanh$ activation function together with different training objectives, such as residual or energy minimization. The key idea is to augment the approximation space with suitable exponential functions, similar to enriched spaces in finite element methods, e.g.~\cite{Kellogg}. Numerical examples in one and two dimensions, including a smooth non-tensor-product domain, illustrate the resulting parameter-robust behavior over the tested perturbation ranges.

</details>


### [107] [A Generalized Scalar Auxiliary Variable Method for Structure-Preserving and Efficient Integration of Nonlinear port-Hamiltonian DAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05246)
*Aashutosh Sharma,Andreas Bartel,Manuel Schaller*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个能量最优的广义标量辅助变量（EOP-GSAV）框架，用于求解非线性指标一的端口-哈密顿微分代数方程（pH-DAEs）。基于BDF-1和BDF-2格式，每时间步仅需一次线性求解且因子可复用，同时保持离散无源性和哈密顿量精确跟踪。实验表明该方法在匹配精度场景下比隐式中点法（含多种牛顿迭代变体）效率更高，与SUNDIALS IDA相比在同阶情况下具有可比的功-精度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非线性指标一的端口-哈密顿微分代数方程（pH-DAEs），现有数值方法（如隐式中点法配合牛顿迭代）通常需要多轮非线性迭代，计算成本高且难以在保证结构保持（离散无源性与哈密顿量守恒）的同时实现高效求解。作者希望开发一种既能保持端口-哈密顿结构性质、又能在每时间步仅需一次可复用因式分解的线性求解的高效数值格式。

Method: 利用端口-哈密顿结构，将非线性努力项、互联项和耗散项从恒定隐式核心中分离出来，构建能量最优的广义标量辅助变量（EOP-GSAV）框架。在此框架下设计BDF-1和BDF-2离散格式，每个时间步仅需一次线性系统求解且系数矩阵因子可复用。通过数值实验与隐式中点法（配合全雅可比、修正雅可比和冻结雅可比牛顿迭代）以及SUNDIALS IDA求解器进行对比验证。

Result: 在从强状态依赖的非线性应力测试到大规模基准问题的数值实验中，该方法展现出稳健且有竞争力的性能，在匹配精度场景下具有显著的效率提升。与SUNDIALS IDA的比较显示，即使基于非专业化的Python/SciPy实现，在相同阶数下也表现出可比的功-精度行为；在高精度区域，不受限的变阶自适应IDA运行更快。

Conclusion: EOP-GSAV框架为非线性pH-DAEs提供了一种高效、结构保持的数值求解方案，兼具单次线性求解的计算优势与离散无源性/哈密顿量跟踪等结构保留特性，是现有隐式方法和通用DAE求解器的一个有竞争力的替代方案。

Abstract: We develop an energy-optimal generalized scalar auxiliary variable (EOP-GSAV) framework for nonlinear index-one port-Hamiltonian differential-algebraic equations (pH-DAEs). Exploiting the port-Hamiltonian structure, we separate the nonlinear effort, interconnection, and dissipation terms from a constant implicit core. The resulting BDF-1 and BDF-2 schemes require one linear solve per time step with a reusable factorization, while retaining discrete passivity and accurate tracking of the Hamiltonian. The schemes are compared with the implicit midpoint method equipped with full, modified, and frozen-Jacobian Newton iterations. Numerical experiments ranging from a strongly state-dependent nonlinear stress test to large-scale benchmarks demonstrate robust and competitive performance, with substantial efficiency gains in matched-accuracy regimes. A comparison with SUNDIALS IDA shows comparable work-precision behavior at equal order despite a non-specialized Python/SciPy implementation, while unrestricted variable-order adaptive IDA is faster in the high-accuracy regime.

</details>


### [108] [Shallow neural network approximation in mixed Sobolev spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05263)
*Yuwen Li,Guozhi Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了浅层神经网络对混合Sobolev空间函数的最佳L2逼近问题。作者建立了与激活函数无关的Fourier-block原理：若激活函数在Fourier-block意义下具有单变量逼近阶ρ，则全局代数逼近率可达min{α,ρ}（对混合光滑度α的目标函数），并给出显式对数因子。针对具体激活函数，文章引入结构化单变量逼近条件来验证Fourier-block性质。对于ReLU^k，匹配的代数下界确认min{α,k+1}是任意维度下的最优代数逼近指数；该框架同样给出基数B样条和soft-ReLU^k的指数min{α,k+1}，以及ELU和余弦激活的完整混合光滑指数α，均至多差对数因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解浅层神经网络在逼近高维混合光滑函数时的最优逼近率是一个基础理论问题。已有工作对不同激活函数给出分散的结果，缺乏统一的理论框架来刻画激活函数性质与最终逼近阶之间的精确关系。本文旨在建立一个普遍原理，将激活函数的单变量逼近性质与全局多变量逼近速率系统地联系起来，从而统一并推广已有结果。

Method: 首先建立激活无关的Fourier-block原理：利用调和分析中的Fourier块分解技术，将目标函数的混合光滑度α与激活函数的单变量逼近阶ρ结合，得到全局逼近率min{α,ρ}（含对数因子）。其次引入一种结构化单变量逼近条件，该条件足以蕴含Fourier-block性质并给出显式参数。最后对ReLU^k推导匹配的代数下界，验证指数的最优性，并将框架应用于B样条、soft-ReLU^k、ELU及余弦等具体激活。

Result: (1) 建立Fourier-block原理：全局代数逼近阶为min{α,ρ}（至多差对数因子）；(2) 结构化单变量逼近条件可验证各类激活的Fourier-block性质；(3) 对ReLU^k证明min{α,k+1}是任意维度下的最优代数逼近指数（下界匹配上界）；(4) 将相同指数推广到基数B样条和soft-ReLU^k；(5) 对ELU和余弦激活证明可达完整混合光滑指数α（至多差对数因子）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统一的逼近理论框架，通过Fourier-block原理将激活函数的单变量逼近阶与混合Sobolev空间函数的全局逼近率精确关联。ReLU^k、B样条等非光滑激活受限于min{α,k+1}，而ELU、余弦等充分光滑激活可达到最优混合光滑指数α。该结果系统性地回答了不同激活函数在浅层网络逼近中的能力边界问题。

Abstract: We investigate the best $L_2$ approximation of mixed Sobolev spaces by shallow neural networks with $n$ neurons and general activation functions. We first establish an activation-independent Fourier-block principle: if an activation has univariate approximation order $ρ$ in the sense of the Fourier-block property, then the global approximation rate has algebraic order $\min\{α,ρ\}$ for target functions of mixed smoothness $α$, up to explicit logarithmic factors. To verify this property for concrete activations, we introduce a structured univariate approximation condition that implies the Fourier-block property with explicit parameters. For $\mathrm{ReLU}^k$, a matching algebraic lower bound identifies $\min\{α,k+1\}$ as the optimal algebraic approximation exponent in any dimension, up to logarithmic factors in the upper bound. The framework also yields the exponent $\min\{α,k+1\}$ for cardinal B-splines and soft-$\mathrm{ReLU}^k$, and the full mixed-smoothness exponent $α$ for ELU and cosine activations, again up to logarithmic~factors.

</details>

### [109] [Derived Associative Algebras and Cyclic Cohomology of Q-manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04805)
*Andrew James Bruce*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Q-流形（配备"平方为零"奇向量场的超流形）的框架下重新审视了Loday的派生积。通过移位次数将Grassmann奇乘积解释为标准乘积，证明任何Q-流形都伴随一个派生的（非交换）结合Z₂-分次代数。将Connes循环上同调应用于该派生结合代数，得到Q-流形上区别于标准上同调的新上同调——派生循环上同调。该派生循环余循环在BV–BFV–BRST形式中被解释为经典BRST不变泛函，或在正则叶状结构情形下经由叶状李代数胚被解释为广义Ruelle–Sullivan流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望将Loday在非交换几何中引入的派生积（derived product）与Q-流形这一逐步发展中的重要几何对象相结合，通过新的代数结构揭示Q-流形上的隐藏上同调不变量，并建立其与BRST量子化及叶状结构几何中的物理与拓扑不变量之间的联系。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 通过移位分次将奇乘积重新解释为标准结合乘积，从而构造派生结合代数；(2) 将Connes的循环上同调理论移植到该派生结合代数上，定义Q-流形的派生循环上同调；(3) 将该上同调的循环余循环在BRST/BV-BFV框架下或正则叶状结构的李代数胚语境中进行物理解释。

Result: 建立了任意Q-流形上派生结合代数结构的存在性，并由此构造了一种新的上同调理论——派生循环上同调，该理论不依赖于标准Q-流形上同调。所得循环余循环被证明可以精确对应经典BRST不变泛函或广义Ruelle–Sullivan流，从而实现了几何构造与物理规范理论及动力系统不变量之间的对应。

Conclusion: 本文表明Q-流形不仅承载标准的上同调结构，还承载由派生乘积诱导的循环上同调结构。该新理论统一了非交换代数、BRST形式主义与叶状结构几何中的若干概念，为Q-流形的深层次几何与物理研究提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Loday's derived product is revisited in the setting of Q-manifolds, i.e., supermanifolds equipped with an odd vector field that `squares to zero'. We interpret the Grassmann odd product as a standard product upon shifting the grading, which implies the existence of a derived (noncommutative) associative $\mathbb{Z}_2$-graded algebra associated with any Q-manifold. We apply Connes' cyclic cohomology to the derived associative algebra, giving a new cohomology on Q-manifolds distinct from the standard cohomology: we refer to this as the derived cyclic cohomology of a Q-manifold. The derived cyclic cocycles are interpreted as classically BRST-invariant functionals within the BV--BFV--BRST formalism or generalised Ruelle--Sullivan currents when applied to regular foliations via their foliation Lie algebroids.

</details>


### [110] [Finite-Corner Reconstruction and Separation for Cones of Normal Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04889)
*Mohsen Kian,Fuad Kittaneh*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种有限角框架来描述从B(H)到B(K)的正常映射构成的凸锥。在有限截断-填充（cut-pad）运算下稳定的基本结构假设下，证明了每个点-超弱闭的cut-pad稳定锥完全由其有限维角锥决定，反之每个一致的闭有限维角锥族有唯一全局实现。还给出了重建原理和有限维见证的分离结果，并在完全正映射和可分解映射上做了示例说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机是为算子代数之间正常映射所构成的凸锥提供一个系统性的结构描述框架。凸锥在量子信息、算子理论等领域有广泛用途（如完全正映射、可分解映射的锥），而理解这些锥的结构需要一种统一的"有限维截断"描述方法——通过有限维角锥来刻画无穷维全局结构。

Method: 方法核心包括：(1) 引入"cut-pad稳定"的概念作为基本结构假设，即锥在有限截断和填充运算下封闭；(2) 证明点-超弱闭的cut-pad稳定锥与其有限维角锥族之间的双射对应关系；(3) 利用范数闭包重建非闭锥的点-超弱闭包；(4) 结合有限维分离定理和Choi表示，构造有限维见证来检测全局锥的非成员性；(5) 在完全正映射与可分解映射上验证框架，说明有限角见证的埃尔米特部分具有PPT性质。

Result: 论文取得了以下主要结果：点-超弱闭的cut-pad稳定锥与一致闭有限维角锥族之间存在一一对应；任意cut-pad稳定锥可由其角锥的范数闭包重建其点-超弱闭包；非成员性总能在单一有限维角上通过有限维见证被检测；在可分解映射情形下，这些见证的埃尔米特部分是PPT的。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个普适的有限角框架，不仅统一了完全正映射和可分解映射等经典锥的描述，还提供了一套完整的从有限维信息重建无穷维锥结构的理论工具。该框架有望在量子信息理论（如纠缠见证、正映射研究）和算子代数领域产生广泛应用。

Abstract: We develop a finite-corner framework for convex cones of normal maps between \(\mathcal B(\mathcal H)\) and \(\mathcal B(\mathcal K)\). The basic structural assumption is stability under finite cut--pad operations. We prove that every point-ultraweakly closed cut--pad stable cone is completely determined by its finite-dimensional corner cones, and conversely that every coherent family of closed finite-dimensional corner cones admits a unique global realization of this type. For a cut--pad stable cone that is not necessarily point-ultraweakly closed, reconstruction from the norm closures of its corner cones yields exactly its point-ultraweak closure. Combining this reconstruction principle with finite-dimensional separation and the Choi representation, we show that non-membership in a global cone is always detected on a single finite-dimensional corner by a finite-dimensional witness. We illustrate the framework for completely positive and decomposable maps; in the decomposable case, the Hermitian parts of the finite-corner witnesses are PPT.

</details>


### [111] [Representation Theory of Canonical Commutation Relations Arising from the Quantization of the Klein-Gordon Equation with an External Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05237)
*Yasumichi Matsuzawa,Itaru Sasaki,Akito Suzuki*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在外势影响下量子化标量场的Weyl表示问题，证明了当质量改变时Weyl表示必然不等价；质量相同时，等价与不等价的转变点出现在势能差衰减率为-3/2处，而非散射理论中短程条件的-1衰减率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子化标量场在外部势场影响下，当质量和/或势能改变时，Weyl表示（描述正则对易关系的一种数学形式）何时保持等价或变为不等价。这一问题的核心源于量子场论中Gelfand-Naimark-Segal（GNI构造的）等价性判断，涉及可约化表示的结构性理解。

Method: 将Weyl表示等价性判断问题约化为对Schrödinger算子的分析。通过考察质量与势能变化后对应的Schrödinger算子的谱性质及相关的函数空间性质，来判断两种Weyl表示之间是否存在酉等价（即保持正则对易关系的映射）。

Result: 得到了两个关键结论：(1) 当质量不同时，Weyl表示必然不等价；(2) 当质量相同时，等价与不等价之间的临界转变发生在两个势能之差的衰减率为-3/2处，这一结果与散射理论中短程条件所对应的衰减率-1形成了鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了Weyl表示的等价性分类与Schrödinger算子之间深刻的数学联系，并发现了势能衰减率-3/2这一新的临界指标，它不同于散射理论中已知的短程条件临界衰减率-1，暗示量子场论中表示的等价性条件比散射理论中的势分类更为严格。

Abstract: We investigate the Weyl representation of the canonical commutation relations for a model describing a quantized massive scalar field under the influence of an external potential. The main problem is to determine whether the Weyl representation remains equivalent to or becomes inequivalent to the original one when the mass and/or potential are changed. This problem is reduced to the study of Schrödinger operators. It turns out that the Weyl representations are inequivalent when the masses differ. Moreover, when the masses are the same, the transition between equivalence and inequivalence occurs at the decay rate $-3/2$ of the difference between the potentials. This contrasts with the decay rate $-1$ that defines the short-range condition in scattering theory.

</details>
